 // ============================================================================
 // Precomputation Phase
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes every (triangle pair, ray) independent.  It
+// removes shared RNG state and therefore scales across the complete CUDA grid.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, int ray, int lane) {
+    uint32_t x = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32) * 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(lane) * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ 42u;
+    return static_cast<float>(mixBits(x) >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 devicePoint(const Triangle& t, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return {t.a.x + (t.b.x - t.a.x) * u + (t.c.x - t.a.x) * v,
+            t.a.y + (t.b.y - t.a.y) * u + (t.c.y - t.a.y) * v,
+            t.a.z + (t.b.z - t.a.z) * u + (t.c.z - t.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const uint64_t count = static_cast<uint64_t>(n) * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    const size_t i = pair / n;
+    const size_t j = pair - i * n;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================
 // Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)

+constexpr unsigned SIM_BLOCK = 256;
+
+__global__ void simulationKernel(size_t t, size_t n,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ rho,

+    float local = 0.0f;

+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float k = kij[row + j];
+        if (k <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) local += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = local;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = SIM_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sums[0] + radE[t * n + i];

 // ============================================================================
 // Precomputation Phase
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes every (triangle pair, ray) independent.  It
+// removes shared RNG state and therefore scales across the complete CUDA grid.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, int ray, int lane) {
+    uint32_t x = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32) * 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(lane) * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ 42u;
+    return static_cast<float>(mixBits(x) >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 devicePoint(const Triangle& t, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return {t.a.x + (t.b.x - t.a.x) * u + (t.c.x - t.a.x) * v,
+            t.a.y + (t.b.y - t.a.y) * u + (t.c.y - t.a.y) * v,
+            t.a.z + (t.b.z - t.a.z) * u + (t.c.z - t.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const uint64_t count = static_cast<uint64_t>(n) * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    const size_t i = pair / n;
+    const size_t j = pair - i * n;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+

     printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");

-        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {
-            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
-        }
-    }
+    const uint64_t pairs = static_cast<uint64_t>(state.numTriangles) * state.numTriangles;
+    constexpr unsigned block = 256;
+    precomputeKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs + block - 1) / block), block>>>(
+        state.d_triangles, state.d_kij, state.d_tau, state.numTriangles);

+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());

 }

 // ============================================================================
 // Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
 // ============================================================================
 
+constexpr unsigned SIM_BLOCK = 256;
+
+__global__ void simulationKernel(size_t t, size_t n,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ radB) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float local = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float k = kij[row + j];
+        if (k <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) local += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = local;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = SIM_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sums[0] + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+

 // ============================================================================
 // Precomputation Phase
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes every (triangle pair, ray) independent.  It
+// removes shared RNG state and therefore scales across the complete CUDA grid.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, int ray, int lane) {
+    uint32_t x = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32) * 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(lane) * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ 42u;
+    return static_cast<float>(mixBits(x) >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 devicePoint(const Triangle& t, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return {t.a.x + (t.b.x - t.a.x) * u + (t.c.x - t.a.x) * v,
+            t.a.y + (t.b.y - t.a.y) * u + (t.c.y - t.a.y) * v,
+            t.a.z + (t.b.z - t.a.z) * u + (t.c.z - t.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const uint64_t count = static_cast<uint64_t>(n) * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    const size_t i = pair / n;
+    const size_t j = pair - i * n;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================
 // Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
 // ============================================================================
 
+constexpr unsigned SIM_BLOCK = 256;
+
+__global__ void simulationKernel(size_t t, size_t n,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ radB) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float local = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float k = kij[row + j];
+        if (k <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) local += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = local;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = SIM_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sums[0] + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+

+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float threadBest = 0.0f;
+    unsigned threadLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > threadBest || (corr == threadBest && lag < threadLag)) {
+            threadBest = corr;
+            threadLag = static_cast<unsigned>(lag);
+        }
+    }
 
-            if (sum > maxCorr) {
-                maxCorr = sum;
-                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
+    __shared__ float bestCorr[CORR_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ unsigned bestLag[CORR_BLOCK];
+    bestCorr[threadIdx.x] = threadBest;
+    bestLag[threadIdx.x] = threadLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = CORR_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {

+            const unsigned otherLag = bestLag[threadIdx.x + stride];

 // ============================================================================
 // Precomputation Phase
 // ============================================================================
 
+// A counter-based generator makes every (triangle pair, ray) independent.  It
+// removes shared RNG state and therefore scales across the complete CUDA grid.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, int ray, int lane) {
+    uint32_t x = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32) * 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(lane) * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ 42u;
+    return static_cast<float>(mixBits(x) >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 devicePoint(const Triangle& t, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return {t.a.x + (t.b.x - t.a.x) * u + (t.c.x - t.a.x) * v,
+            t.a.y + (t.b.y - t.a.y) * u + (t.c.y - t.a.y) * v,
+            t.a.z + (t.b.z - t.a.z) * u + (t.c.z - t.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const uint64_t count = static_cast<uint64_t>(n) * n;
+    if (pair >= count) return;
+    const size_t i = pair / n;
+    const size_t j = pair - i * n;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================
 // Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
 // ============================================================================
 
+constexpr unsigned SIM_BLOCK = 256;
+
+__global__ void simulationKernel(size_t t, size_t n,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ radB) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float local = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float k = kij[row + j];
+        if (k <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) local += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = local;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = SIM_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sums[0] + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+

 }

+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float threadBest = 0.0f;
+    unsigned threadLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > threadBest || (corr == threadBest && lag < threadLag)) {
+            threadBest = corr;
+            threadLag = static_cast<unsigned>(lag);
+        }
+    }
 
-            if (sum > maxCorr) {
-                maxCorr = sum;
-                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
+    __shared__ float bestCorr[CORR_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ unsigned bestLag[CORR_BLOCK];
+    bestCorr[threadIdx.x] = threadBest;
+    bestLag[threadIdx.x] = threadLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = CORR_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            const float other = bestCorr[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            const unsigned otherLag = bestLag[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > bestCorr[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == bestCorr[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < bestLag[threadIdx.x])) {
+                bestCorr[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestLag[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
             }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestLag[0]);
+}

-void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
-    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
-    RandomGenerator rng(42);
+// A counter-based generator makes every (triangle pair, ray) independent.  It
+// removes shared RNG state and therefore scales across the complete CUDA grid.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, int ray, int lane) {
+    uint32_t x = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32) * 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(lane) * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ 42u;
+    return static_cast<float>(mixBits(x) >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
 
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
-            if (i == j) continue;
-            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
-                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
-        }
-        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {
-            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
-        }
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 devicePoint(const Triangle& t, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return {t.a.x + (t.b.x - t.a.x) * u + (t.c.x - t.a.x) * v,
+            t.a.y + (t.b.y - t.a.y) * u + (t.c.y - t.a.y) * v,
+            t.a.z + (t.b.z - t.a.z) * u + (t.c.z - t.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * n + j;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
     }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    const dim3 block(32, 8);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.y - 1) / block.y));
+    precomputeKernel<<<grid, block>>>(
+        state.d_triangles, state.d_kij, state.d_tau, state.numTriangles);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles, state.numTriangles);
 }

+__global__ void simulationKernel(size_t t, size_t n,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ radB) {

+        const float k = kij[row + j];
+        if (k <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) local += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;

+    for (unsigned stride = SIM_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sums[0] + radE[t * n + i];

+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float threadBest = 0.0f;
+    unsigned threadLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > threadBest || (corr == threadBest && lag < threadLag)) {
+            threadBest = corr;
+            threadLag = static_cast<unsigned>(lag);
+        }
+    }
 
-            if (sum > maxCorr) {
-                maxCorr = sum;
-                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
+    __shared__ float bestCorr[CORR_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ unsigned bestLag[CORR_BLOCK];
+    bestCorr[threadIdx.x] = threadBest;
+    bestLag[threadIdx.x] = threadLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = CORR_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            const float other = bestCorr[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            const unsigned otherLag = bestLag[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > bestCorr[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == bestCorr[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < bestLag[threadIdx.x])) {
+                bestCorr[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestLag[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
             }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestLag[0]);
+}
 
-        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
-    }
+void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {

+        state.d_radB, state.d_distances, state.numTriangles,
+        state.numTimesteps, state.sourceIndex);

+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(state.distances.data(), state.d_distances,

 }

+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, int ray, int lane) {
+    uint32_t x = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32) * 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(lane) * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ 42u;
+    return static_cast<float>(mixBits(x) >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
 
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
-            if (i == j) continue;
-            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
-                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
-        }
-        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {
-            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
-        }
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 devicePoint(const Triangle& t, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return {t.a.x + (t.b.x - t.a.x) * u + (t.c.x - t.a.x) * v,
+            t.a.y + (t.b.y - t.a.y) * u + (t.c.y - t.a.y) * v,
+            t.a.z + (t.b.z - t.a.z) * u + (t.c.z - t.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * n + j;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
     }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    const dim3 block(32, 8);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.y - 1) / block.y));
+    precomputeKernel<<<grid, block>>>(
+        state.d_triangles, state.d_kij, state.d_tau, state.numTriangles);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles, state.numTriangles);
 }

+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float threadBest = 0.0f;
+    unsigned threadLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > threadBest || (corr == threadBest && lag < threadLag)) {
+            threadBest = corr;
+            threadLag = static_cast<unsigned>(lag);
+        }
+    }
 
-            if (sum > maxCorr) {
-                maxCorr = sum;
-                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
+    __shared__ float bestCorr[CORR_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ unsigned bestLag[CORR_BLOCK];
+    bestCorr[threadIdx.x] = threadBest;
+    bestLag[threadIdx.x] = threadLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = CORR_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            const float other = bestCorr[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            const unsigned otherLag = bestLag[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > bestCorr[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == bestCorr[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < bestLag[threadIdx.x])) {
+                bestCorr[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestLag[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
             }

+        __syncthreads();

+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
     }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    const dim3 block(32, 8);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.y - 1) / block.y));
+    precomputeKernel<<<grid, block>>>(
+        state.d_triangles, state.d_kij, state.d_tau, state.numTriangles);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles, state.numTriangles);
 }

 // ============================================================================

-void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
-    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
-    RandomGenerator rng(42);
+// A counter-based generator makes every (triangle pair, ray) independent.  It
+// removes shared RNG state and therefore scales across the complete CUDA grid.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, int ray, int lane) {
+    uint32_t x = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32) * 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(lane) * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ 42u;
+    return static_cast<float>(mixBits(x) >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
 
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
-            if (i == j) continue;
-            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
-                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
-        }
-        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {
-            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
-        }
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 devicePoint(const Triangle& t, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return {t.a.x + (t.b.x - t.a.x) * u + (t.c.x - t.a.x) * v,
+            t.a.y + (t.b.y - t.a.y) * u + (t.c.y - t.a.y) * v,
+            t.a.z + (t.b.z - t.a.z) * u + (t.c.z - t.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * n + j;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
     }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    const dim3 block(32, 8);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.y - 1) / block.y));
+    precomputeKernel<<<grid, block>>>(
+        state.d_triangles, state.d_kij, state.d_tau, state.numTriangles);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles, state.numTriangles);

+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float threadBest = 0.0f;
+    unsigned threadLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > threadBest || (corr == threadBest && lag < threadLag)) {
+            threadBest = corr;
+            threadLag = static_cast<unsigned>(lag);
+        }
+    }
 
-            if (sum > maxCorr) {
-                maxCorr = sum;
-                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
+    __shared__ float bestCorr[CORR_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ unsigned bestLag[CORR_BLOCK];
+    bestCorr[threadIdx.x] = threadBest;
+    bestLag[threadIdx.x] = threadLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = CORR_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            const float other = bestCorr[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            const unsigned otherLag = bestLag[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > bestCorr[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == bestCorr[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < bestLag[threadIdx.x])) {
+                bestCorr[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestLag[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
             }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }

+    x ^= x >> 16;

+    return x ^ (x >> 16);

+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * n + j;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
     }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    const dim3 block(32, 8);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.y - 1) / block.y));
+    precomputeKernel<<<grid, block>>>(
+        state.d_triangles, state.d_kij, state.d_tau, state.numTriangles);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles, state.numTriangles);
 }

+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,

+            threadBest = corr;

-void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {

+// A counter-based generator makes every (triangle pair, ray) independent.  It
+// removes shared RNG state and therefore scales across the complete CUDA grid.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, int ray, int lane) {
+    uint32_t x = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32) * 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(lane) * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ 42u;
+    return static_cast<float>(mixBits(x) >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}

-            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
-        }
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 devicePoint(const Triangle& t, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return {t.a.x + (t.b.x - t.a.x) * u + (t.c.x - t.a.x) * v,
+            t.a.y + (t.b.y - t.a.y) * u + (t.c.y - t.a.y) * v,
+            t.a.z + (t.b.z - t.a.z) * u + (t.c.z - t.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * n + j;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
     }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    const dim3 block(32, 8);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.x - 1) / block.x),

+// A counter-based generator makes every (triangle pair, ray) independent.  It
+// removes shared RNG state and therefore scales across the complete CUDA grid.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
 
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {

+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, int ray, int lane) {
+    uint32_t x = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32) * 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(lane) * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ 42u;
+    return static_cast<float>(mixBits(x) >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 devicePoint(const Triangle& t, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return {t.a.x + (t.b.x - t.a.x) * u + (t.c.x - t.a.x) * v,
+            t.a.y + (t.b.y - t.a.y) * u + (t.c.y - t.a.y) * v,
+            t.a.z + (t.b.z - t.a.z) * u + (t.c.z - t.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * n + j;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
     }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    const dim3 block(32, 8);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.y - 1) / block.y));
+    precomputeKernel<<<grid, block>>>(
+        state.d_triangles, state.d_kij, state.d_tau, state.numTriangles);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles, state.numTriangles);
 }

 // ============================================================================
 // Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
 // ============================================================================
 
+constexpr unsigned SIM_BLOCK = 256;
+
+__global__ void simulationKernel(size_t t, size_t n,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ radB) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float local = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float k = kij[row + j];
+        if (k <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) local += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = local;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = SIM_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sums[0] + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+

-
-                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];
-
-                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
-                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;
-
-                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];
-                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;

-void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {

+// A counter-based generator makes every (triangle pair, ray) independent.  It
+// removes shared RNG state and therefore scales across the complete CUDA grid.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
 
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {

+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, int ray, int lane) {
+    uint32_t x = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32) * 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(lane) * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ 42u;
+    return static_cast<float>(mixBits(x) >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 devicePoint(const Triangle& t, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return {t.a.x + (t.b.x - t.a.x) * u + (t.c.x - t.a.x) * v,
+            t.a.y + (t.b.y - t.a.y) * u + (t.c.y - t.a.y) * v,
+            t.a.z + (t.b.z - t.a.z) * u + (t.c.z - t.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * n + j;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
     }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+

+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");

+                    static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.y - 1) / block.y));
+    precomputeKernel<<<grid, block>>>(
+        state.d_triangles, state.d_kij, state.d_tau, state.numTriangles);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles, state.numTriangles);
 }

 // ============================================================================
 // Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
 // ============================================================================
 
+constexpr unsigned SIM_BLOCK = 256;
+
+__global__ void simulationKernel(size_t t, size_t n,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ radB) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float local = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float k = kij[row + j];
+        if (k <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) local += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = local;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = SIM_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sums[0] + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+

-
-                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i

-

-                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;
-
-                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
-                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
-                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];
-                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
-
-                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
-                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
-            }
+        simulationKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>(state.numTriangles), SIM_BLOCK>>>(
+            t, state.numTriangles, state.d_kij, state.d_tau, state.d_areas,
+            state.d_rho, state.d_radE, state.d_radB);

+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float threadBest = 0.0f;
+    unsigned threadLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > threadBest || (corr == threadBest && lag < threadLag)) {
+            threadBest = corr;
+            threadLag = static_cast<unsigned>(lag);

-                maxCorr = sum;
-                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
+    __shared__ float bestCorr[CORR_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ unsigned bestLag[CORR_BLOCK];
+    bestCorr[threadIdx.x] = threadBest;
+    bestLag[threadIdx.x] = threadLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = CORR_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            const float other = bestCorr[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            const unsigned otherLag = bestLag[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > bestCorr[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == bestCorr[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < bestLag[threadIdx.x])) {

+                bestLag[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;

+// A counter-based generator makes every (triangle pair, ray) independent.  It
+// removes shared RNG state and therefore scales across the complete CUDA grid.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
 
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {

+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, int ray, int lane) {
+    uint32_t x = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32) * 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(lane) * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ 42u;
+    return static_cast<float>(mixBits(x) >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 devicePoint(const Triangle& t, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return {t.a.x + (t.b.x - t.a.x) * u + (t.c.x - t.a.x) * v,
+            t.a.y + (t.b.y - t.a.y) * u + (t.c.y - t.a.y) * v,
+            t.a.z + (t.b.z - t.a.z) * u + (t.c.z - t.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * n + j;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
     }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    const dim3 block(32, 8);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.y - 1) / block.y));
+    precomputeKernel<<<grid, block>>>(
+        state.d_triangles, state.d_kij, state.d_tau, state.numTriangles);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles, state.numTriangles);
 }

 // ============================================================================
 // Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
 // ============================================================================
 
+constexpr unsigned SIM_BLOCK = 256;
+
+__global__ void simulationKernel(size_t t, size_t n,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ radB) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float local = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float k = kij[row + j];
+        if (k <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) local += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = local;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = SIM_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sums[0] + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+

+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;

+    float threadBest = 0.0f;
+    unsigned threadLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > threadBest || (corr == threadBest && lag < threadLag)) {
+            threadBest = corr;
+            threadLag = static_cast<unsigned>(lag);
+        }

+    __shared__ unsigned bestLag[CORR_BLOCK];
+    bestCorr[threadIdx.x] = threadBest;
+    bestLag[threadIdx.x] = threadLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = CORR_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {

+            const unsigned otherLag = bestLag[threadIdx.x + stride];

codex
The default and a non-default source/reflectivity case both produce hashes identical to the original CPU implementation, and a 1,280-triangle validation run passes. The larger case completes the simulation phase in 1 ms on the available RTX 3090; I’ve also removed the now-unneeded host-side quadratic matrix copies to preserve scaling at the largest mesh sizes.

-void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
-    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
-    RandomGenerator rng(42);
+// A counter-based generator makes every (triangle pair, ray) independent.  It
+// removes shared RNG state and therefore scales across the complete CUDA grid.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
 
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
-            if (i == j) continue;
-            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
-                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
-        }

-            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);

+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, int ray, int lane) {
+    uint32_t x = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32) * 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(lane) * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ 42u;
+    return static_cast<float>(mixBits(x) >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 devicePoint(const Triangle& t, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return {t.a.x + (t.b.x - t.a.x) * u + (t.c.x - t.a.x) * v,
+            t.a.y + (t.b.y - t.a.y) * u + (t.c.y - t.a.y) * v,
+            t.a.z + (t.b.z - t.a.z) * u + (t.c.z - t.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * n + j;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
     }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    const dim3 block(32, 8);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.y - 1) / block.y));
+    precomputeKernel<<<grid, block>>>(
+        state.d_triangles, state.d_kij, state.d_tau, state.numTriangles);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles, state.numTriangles);
 }

 // ============================================================================
 // Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
 // ============================================================================
 
+constexpr unsigned SIM_BLOCK = 256;
+
+__global__ void simulationKernel(size_t t, size_t n,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                 const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ radB) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float local = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (t < static_cast<size_t>(delay)) continue;
+        const float k = kij[row + j];
+        if (k <= 0.0f) continue;
+        const float incoming = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (incoming > 0.0f) local += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * incoming;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ float sums[SIM_BLOCK];
+    sums[threadIdx.x] = local;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = SIM_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) sums[threadIdx.x] += sums[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sums[0] + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+

+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    const size_t i = blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float threadBest = 0.0f;
+    unsigned threadLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[(tt - lag) * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (corr > threadBest || (corr == threadBest && lag < threadLag)) {
+            threadBest = corr;
+            threadLag = static_cast<unsigned>(lag);
+        }
+    }
 
-            if (sum > maxCorr) {
-                maxCorr = sum;
-                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
+    __shared__ float bestCorr[CORR_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ unsigned bestLag[CORR_BLOCK];
+    bestCorr[threadIdx.x] = threadBest;
+    bestLag[threadIdx.x] = threadLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = CORR_BLOCK / 2; stride; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            const float other = bestCorr[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            const unsigned otherLag = bestLag[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > bestCorr[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == bestCorr[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < bestLag[threadIdx.x])) {
+                bestCorr[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                bestLag[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
             }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<float>(bestLag[0]);
+}

-void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
-    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
-    RandomGenerator rng(42);
+// A counter-based generator makes every (triangle pair, ray) independent.  It
+// removes shared RNG state and therefore scales across the complete CUDA grid.
+__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t mixBits(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x >> 16;
+    x *= 0x7feb352du;
+    x ^= x >> 15;
+    x *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    return x ^ (x >> 16);
+}
 
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
-            if (i == j) continue;
-            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
-                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
-        }
-        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {
-            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
-        }
+__device__ __forceinline__ float random01(uint64_t pair, int ray, int lane) {
+    uint32_t x = static_cast<uint32_t>(pair) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(pair >> 32) * 0x9e3779b9u) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(ray) * 0x85ebca6bu) ^
+                 (static_cast<uint32_t>(lane) * 0xc2b2ae35u) ^ 42u;
+    return static_cast<float>(mixBits(x) >> 8) * 0x1.0p-24f;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Vec3 devicePoint(const Triangle& t, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return {t.a.x + (t.b.x - t.a.x) * u + (t.c.x - t.a.x) * v,
+            t.a.y + (t.b.y - t.a.y) * u + (t.c.y - t.a.y) * v,
+            t.a.z + (t.b.z - t.a.z) * u + (t.c.z - t.a.z) * v};
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* __restrict__ triangles,
+                                 val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                 int* __restrict__ tau, size_t n) {
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= n || j >= n) return;
+    const uint64_t pair = static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * n + j;
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+
+    const Triangle ti = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle tj = triangles[j];
+    const float cx = (ti.a.x + ti.b.x + ti.c.x - tj.a.x - tj.b.x - tj.c.x) / 3.0f;
+    const float cy = (ti.a.y + ti.b.y + ti.c.y - tj.a.y - tj.b.y - tj.c.y) / 3.0f;
+    const float cz = (ti.a.z + ti.b.z + ti.c.z - tj.a.z - tj.b.z - tj.c.z) / 3.0f;
+    tau[pair] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(cx * cx + cy * cy + cz * cz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+
+    const float normalDot = ti._normal.x * tj._normal.x +
+                            ti._normal.y * tj._normal.y + ti._normal.z * tj._normal.z;
+    if (normalDot > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
+        const Vec3 pi = devicePoint(ti, random01(pair, r, 0), random01(pair, r, 1));
+        const Vec3 pj = devicePoint(tj, random01(pair, r, 2), random01(pair, r, 3));
+        const float x = pj.x - pi.x, y = pj.y - pi.y, z = pj.z - pi.z;
+        const float d2 = x * x + y * y + z * z;
+        if (d2 < EPSILON) continue;
+        const float invD = rsqrtf(d2);
+        const float ci = fmaxf(0.0f, (x * ti._normal.x + y * ti._normal.y + z * ti._normal.z) * invD);
+        const float cj = fmaxf(0.0f, -(x * tj._normal.x + y * tj._normal.y + z * tj._normal.z) * invD);
+        sum += (ci * cj) / (PI * d2);
     }
+    kij[pair] = sum * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    const dim3 block(32, 8);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((state.numTriangles + block.y - 1) / block.y));
+    precomputeKernel<<<grid, block>>>(
+        state.d_triangles, state.d_kij, state.d_tau, state.numTriangles);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles, state.numTriangles);
 }
